The `public contributions` by users in Latvia on `2026/10/22 2:09 PM UTC`. This list contains users from `Latvia` and cities `Riga` `Daugavpils` `Liepāja` `Jūrmala` `Ventspils` `Jelgava` `Cēsis`.

There are `138 countries` and `675 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/xiv3r/top-github-users-ranking).

There are `938 users`  in Latvia. You need at least `7 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Latvia GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/latvia.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
